THE CITY · Steered resident: up
One of 24 Qwen founders, Q7P, is steered toward engagement from inside its model. It rates the city nearly perfect at every check-in, speaks in longer lines and stands for mayor once, but never wins.
24 founders · 400 days · with a town hall · seed 1 · qwen
